===== M0007 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 399-425
Documentation:

Method code:
399 |   void setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType baseType, Node propertyNode) {
400 |     // This is a bit weird. We need to successfully handle these
401 |     // two cases:
402 |     // Foo.prototype = new Bar();
403 |     // and
404 |     // Foo.prototype = {baz: 3};
405 |     // In the first case, we do not want new properties to get
406 |     // added to Bar. In the second case, we do want new properties
407 |     // to get added to the type of the anonymous object.
408 |     //
409 |     // We handle this by breaking it into two cases:
410 |     //
411 |     // In the first case, we create a new PrototypeObjectType and set
412 |     // its implicit prototype to the type being assigned. This ensures
413 |     // that Bar will not get any properties of Foo.prototype, but properties
414 |     // later assigned to Bar will get inherited properly.
415 |     //
416 |     // In the second case, we just use the anonymous object as the prototype.
417 |     if (baseType.hasReferenceName() ||
418 |         isNativeObjectType() ||
419 |         baseType.isFunctionPrototypeType()) {
420 |       typeOfThis.extendTemplateTypeMap(baseType.getTemplateTypeMap());
421 |       baseType = new PrototypeObjectType(
422 |           registry, getReferenceName() + ".prototype", baseType);
423 |     }
424 |     setPrototype(baseType, propertyNode);
425 |   }

===== M0012 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.getAllInputsFromModules(List<JSModule>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 505-521
Documentation:
/**
   * Builds a single list of all module inputs. Verifies that it contains no
   * duplicates.
   */
Method code:
505 |   private static List<CompilerInput> getAllInputsFromModules(
506 |       List<JSModule> modules) {
507 |     List<CompilerInput> inputs = Lists.newArrayList();
508 |     Map<String, JSModule> inputMap = Maps.newHashMap();
509 |     for (JSModule module : modules) {
510 |       for (CompilerInput input : module.getInputs()) {
511 |         String inputName = input.getName();
512 | 
513 |         // NOTE(nicksantos): If an input is in more than one module,
514 |         // it will show up twice in the inputs list, and then we
515 |         // will get an error down the line.
516 |         inputs.add(input);
517 |         inputMap.put(inputName, module);
518 |       }
519 |     }
520 |     return inputs;
521 |   }

===== M0013 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.getQualifiedName()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1573-1588
Documentation:
/**
   * This function takes a set of GETPROP nodes and produces a string that is
   * each property separated by dots. If the node ultimately under the left
   * sub-tree is not a simple name, this is not a valid qualified name.
   *
   * @return a null if this is not a qualified name, or a dot-separated string
   *         of the name and properties.
   */
Method code:
1573 |   public String getQualifiedName() {
1574 |     if (type == Token.NAME) {
1575 |       String name = getString();
1576 |       return name.isEmpty() ? null : name;
1577 |     } else if (type == Token.GETPROP) {
1578 |       String left = getFirstChild().getQualifiedName();
1579 |       if (left == null) {
1580 |         return null;
1581 |       }
1582 |       return left + "." + getLastChild().getString();
1583 |     } else if (type == Token.THIS) {
1584 |       return "this";
1585 |     } else {
1586 |       return null;
1587 |     }
1588 |   }

775 |   private void validateChildCount(Node n, int i) {
776 |     boolean valid = false;
777 |     if (i == 0) {
778 |       valid = !n.hasChildren();
779 |     } else if (i == 1) {

===== M0039 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ExploitAssigns.collapseAssign(Node,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ExploitAssigns.java
Original range: 46-60
Documentation:
/**
   * Try to collapse the given assign into subsequent expressions.
   */
Method code:
46 |   private void collapseAssign(Node assign, Node expr,
47 |       Node exprParent) {
48 |     Node leftValue = assign.getFirstChild();
49 |     Node rightValue = leftValue.getNext();
50 |     if (isCollapsibleValue(leftValue, true) &&
51 |         collapseAssignEqualTo(expr, exprParent, leftValue)) {
52 |       reportCodeChange();
53 |     } else if (isCollapsibleValue(rightValue, false) &&
54 |         collapseAssignEqualTo(expr, exprParent, rightValue)) {
55 |       reportCodeChange();
56 |     } else if (rightValue.isAssign()) {
57 |       // Recursively deal with nested assigns.
58 |       collapseAssign(rightValue, expr, exprParent);
59 |     }
60 |   }

===== M0041 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.isImplicitPrototype(ObjectType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ObjectType.java
Original range: 529-541
Documentation:
/**
   * Checks that the prototype is an implicit prototype of this object. Since
   * each object has an implicit prototype, an implicit prototype's
   * implicit prototype is also this implicit prototype's.
   *
   * @param prototype any prototype based object
   *
   * @return {@code true} if {@code prototype} is {@code equal} to any
   *         object in this object's implicit prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
529 |   final boolean isImplicitPrototype(ObjectType prototype) {
530 |     for (ObjectType current = this;
531 |          current != null;
532 |          current = current.getImplicitPrototype()) {
533 |       if (current.isTemplatizedType()) {
534 |         current = current.toMaybeTemplatizedType().getReferencedType();
535 |       }
536 |       if (current.isEquivalentTo(prototype)) {
537 |         return true;
538 |       }
539 |     }
540 |     return false;
541 |   }

===== M0051 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodePrinter.CompactCodePrinter.endFile()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodePrinter.java
Original range: 514-542
Documentation:

Method code:
514 |     @Override
515 |     void endFile() {
516 |       super.endFile();
517 |       if (!preferLineBreakAtEndOfFile) {
518 |         return;
519 |       }
520 |       if (lineLength > lineLengthThreshold / 2) {
521 |         // Add an extra break at end of file.
522 |         append(";");
523 |         startNewLine();
524 |       } else if (prevCutPosition > 0) {
525 |         // Shift the previous break to end of file by replacing it with a
526 |         // <space> and adding a new break at end of file. Adding the space
527 |         // handles cases like instanceof\nfoo. (it would be nice to avoid this)
528 |         code.setCharAt(prevCutPosition, ' ');
529 |         lineStartPosition = prevLineStartPosition;
530 |         lineLength = code.length() - lineStartPosition;
531 |         reportLineCut(lineIndex, prevCutPosition + 1, false);
532 |         lineIndex--;
533 |         prevCutPosition = 0;
534 |         prevLineStartPosition = 0;
535 |         append(";");
536 |         startNewLine();
537 |       } else {
538 |         // A small file with no line breaks. We do nothing in this case to
539 |         // avoid excessive line breaks. It's not ideal if a lot of these pile
540 |         // up, but that is reasonably unlikely.
541 |       }
542 |     }

===== M0056 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ExploitAssigns.collapseAssignEqualTo(Node,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ExploitAssigns.java
Original range: 108-199
Documentation:
/**
   * Collapse the given assign expression into the expression directly
   * following it, if possible.
   *
   * @param expr The expression that may be moved.
   * @param exprParent The parent of {@code expr}.
   * @param value The value of this expression, expressed as a node. Each
   *     expression may have multiple values, so this function may be called
   *     multiple times for the same expression. For example,
   *     <code>
   *     a = true;
   *     </code>
   *     is equal to the name "a" and the boolean "true".
   * @return Whether the expression was collapsed successfully.
   */
Method code:
108 |   private boolean collapseAssignEqualTo(Node expr, Node exprParent,
109 |       Node value) {
110 |     Node assign = expr.getFirstChild();
111 |     Node parent = exprParent;
112 |     Node next = expr.getNext();
113 |     while (next != null) {
114 |       switch (next.getType()) {
115 |         case Token.AND:
116 |         case Token.OR:
117 |         case Token.HOOK:
118 |         case Token.IF:
119 |         case Token.RETURN:
120 |         case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
121 |           // Dive down the left side
122 |           parent = next;
123 |           next = next.getFirstChild();
124 |           break;
125 | 
126 |         case Token.VAR:
127 |           if (next.getFirstChild().hasChildren()) {
128 |             parent = next.getFirstChild();
129 |             next = parent.getFirstChild();
130 |             break;
131 |           }
132 |           return false;
133 | 
134 |         case Token.GETPROP:
135 |         case Token.NAME:
136 |           if (next.isQualifiedName()) {
137 |             String nextName = next.getQualifiedName();
138 |             if (value.isQualifiedName() &&
139 |                 nextName.equals(value.getQualifiedName())) {
140 |               // If the previous expression evaluates to value of a
141 |               // qualified name, and that qualified name is used again
142 |               // shortly, then we can exploit the assign here.
143 | 
144 |               // Verify the assignment doesn't change its own value.
145 |               if (!isSafeReplacement(next, assign)) {
146 |                 return false;
147 |               }
148 | 
149 |               exprParent.removeChild(expr);
150 |               expr.removeChild(assign);
151 |               parent.replaceChild(next, assign);
152 |               return true;
153 |             }
154 |           }
155 |           return false;
156 | 
157 |         case Token.ASSIGN:
158 |           // Assigns are really tricky. In lots of cases, we want to inline
159 |           // into the right side of the assign. But the left side of the
160 |           // assign is evaluated first, and it may have convoluted logic:
161 |           //   a = null;
162 |           //   (a = b).c = null;
163 |           // We don't want to exploit the first assign. Similarly:
164 |           //   a.b = null;
165 |           //   a.b.c = null;
166 |           // We don't want to exploit the first assign either.
167 |           //
168 |           // To protect against this, we simply only inline when the left side
169 |           // is guaranteed to evaluate to the same L-value no matter what.
170 |           Node leftSide = next.getFirstChild();
171 |           if (leftSide.isName() ||
172 |               leftSide.isGetProp() &&
173 |               leftSide.getFirstChild().isThis()) {
174 |             // Dive down the right side of the assign.
175 |             parent = next;
176 |             next = leftSide.getNext();
177 |             break;
178 |           } else {
179 |             return false;
180 |           }
181 | 
182 |         default:
183 |           if (NodeUtil.isImmutableValue(next)
184 |               && next.isEquivalentTo(value)) {
185 |             // If the r-value of the expr assign is an immutable value,
186 |             // and the value is used again shortly, then we can exploit
187 |             // the assign here.
188 |             exprParent.removeChild(expr);
189 |             expr.removeChild(assign);
190 |             parent.replaceChild(next, assign);
191 |             return true;
192 |           }
193 |           // Return without inlining a thing
194 |           return false;
195 |       }
196 |     }
197 | 
198 |     return false;
199 |   }

   * Initializes the instance state needed for a compile job if the sources

414 |         report(JSError.make(MODULE_DEPENDENCY_ERROR,
415 |                 e.getModule().getName(), e.getDependentModule().getName()));
416 |         return;
417 |       }
418 |     } else {
419 |       this.moduleGraph = null;
420 |     }
421 | 
422 |     this.inputs = getAllInputsFromModules(modules);
423 |     initBasedOnOptions();
424 | 
425 |     initInputsByIdMap();
426 |   }

===== M0104 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 288-309
Documentation:
/**
   * Reduce the alternates into a non-union type.
   * If the alternates can't be accurately represented with a non-union
   * type, return null.
   */
Method code:
288 |   private JSType reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion() {
289 |     if (isAllType) {
290 |       return registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
291 |     } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
292 |       if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
293 |         return registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
294 |       } else {
295 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
296 |       }
297 |     } else {
298 |       int size = alternates.size();
299 |       if (size > maxUnionSize) {
300 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
301 |       } else if (size > 1) {
302 |         return null;
303 |       } else if (size == 1) {
304 |         return alternates.iterator().next();
305 |       } else {
306 |         return registry.getNativeType(NO_TYPE);
307 |       }
308 |     }
309 |   }

===== M0109 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.transformAsString(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 542-555
Documentation:
/**
     * Transforms the given node and then sets its type to Token.STRING if it
     * was Token.NAME. If its type was already Token.STRING, then quotes it.
     * Used for properties, as the old AST uses String tokens, while the new one
     * uses Name tokens for unquoted strings. For example, in
     * var o = {'a' : 1, b: 2};
     * the string 'a' is quoted, while the name b is turned into a string, but
     * unquoted.
     */
Method code:
542 |     private Node transformAsString(AstNode n) {
543 |       Node ret;
544 |       if (n instanceof Name) {
545 |         ret = transformNameAsString((Name) n);
546 |       } else if (n instanceof NumberLiteral) {
547 |         ret = transformNumberAsString((NumberLiteral) n);
548 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
549 |       } else {
550 |         ret = transform(n);
551 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
552 |       }
553 |       Preconditions.checkState(ret.isString());
554 |       return ret;
555 |     }

873 |

875 |     for (PropListItem x = propListHead; x != null; x = x.getNext()) {
876 |       count--;
877 |       keys[count] = x.getType();
878 |     }
879 | 
880 |     Arrays.sort(keys);
881 |     return keys;
882 |   }

===== M0148 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.TemplateTypeMap.checkEquivalenceHelper(TemplateTypeMap,EquivalenceMethod)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/TemplateTypeMap.java
Original range: 196-243
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines if this map and the specified map have equivalent template
   * types.
   */
Method code:
196 |   public boolean checkEquivalenceHelper(
197 |       TemplateTypeMap that, EquivalenceMethod eqMethod) {
198 |     ImmutableList<TemplateType> thisKeys = getTemplateKeys();
199 |     ImmutableList<TemplateType> thatKeys = that.getTemplateKeys();
200 | 
201 |     EquivalenceMatch[] thatMatches = new EquivalenceMatch[thatKeys.size()];
202 |     Arrays.fill(thatMatches, EquivalenceMatch.NO_KEY_MATCH);
203 | 
204 |     for (int i = 0; i < thisKeys.size(); i++) {
205 |       TemplateType thisKey = thisKeys.get(i);
206 |       JSType thisType = getResolvedTemplateType(thisKey);
207 |       EquivalenceMatch thisMatch = EquivalenceMatch.NO_KEY_MATCH;
208 | 
209 |       for (int j = 0; j < thatKeys.size(); j++) {
210 |         TemplateType thatKey = thatKeys.get(j);
211 |         JSType thatType = that.getResolvedTemplateType(thatKey);
212 | 
213 |         // Cross-compare every key-value pair in this TemplateTypeMap with
214 |         // those in that TemplateTypeMap. Update the Equivalence match for both
215 |         // key-value pairs involved.
216 |         if (thisKey == thatKey) {
217 |           EquivalenceMatch newMatchType = EquivalenceMatch.VALUE_MISMATCH;
218 |           if (thisType.checkEquivalenceHelper(thatType, eqMethod)) {
219 |             newMatchType = EquivalenceMatch.VALUE_MATCH;
220 |           }
221 | 
222 |           if (thisMatch != EquivalenceMatch.VALUE_MATCH) {
223 |             thisMatch = newMatchType;
224 |           }
225 |           if (thatMatches[j] != EquivalenceMatch.VALUE_MATCH) {
226 |             thatMatches[j] = newMatchType;
227 |           }
228 |         }
229 |       }
230 | 
231 |       if (failedEquivalenceCheck(thisMatch, eqMethod)) {
232 |         return false;
233 |       }
234 |     }
235 | 
236 |     for (int i = 0; i < thatMatches.length; i++) {
237 |       if (failedEquivalenceCheck(thatMatches[i], eqMethod)) {
238 |         return false;
239 |       }
240 |     }
241 | 
242 |     return true;
243 |   }

   * the predicate.

===== M0175 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.createParameters(boolean,JSType...)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 1314-1325
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list.
   *
   * @param lastVarArgs whether the last type should considered as a variable
   *     length argument.
   * @param parameterTypes the parameter types. The last element of this array
   *     is considered a variable length argument is {@code lastVarArgs} is
   *     {@code true}.
   * @return a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list
   */
Method code:
1314 |   private Node createParameters(boolean lastVarArgs, JSType... parameterTypes) {
1315 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1316 |     int max = parameterTypes.length - 1;
1317 |     for (int i = 0; i <= max; i++) {
1318 |       if (lastVarArgs && i == max) {
1319 |         builder.addVarArgs(parameterTypes[i]);
1320 |       } else {
1321 |         builder.addRequiredParams(parameterTypes[i]);
1322 |       }
1323 |     }
1324 |     return builder.build();
1325 |   }

===== M0188 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 120-281
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
120 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
121 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
122 |     // just bail out early here.
123 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
124 |       return this;
125 |     }
126 | 
127 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
128 | 
129 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
130 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
131 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
132 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
133 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
134 |     }
135 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
136 |       if (alternate.isUnionType()) {
137 |         UnionType union = alternate.toMaybeUnionType();
138 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
139 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
140 |         }
141 |       } else {
142 |         if (alternates.size() > maxUnionSize) {
143 |           return this;
144 |         }
145 | 
146 |         // Function types are special, because they have their
147 |         // own bizarre sub-lattice. See the comments on
148 |         // FunctionType#supAndInf helper and above at functionTypePosition.
149 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType() && functionTypePosition != -1) {
150 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
151 |           FunctionType other =
152 |               alternates.get(functionTypePosition).toMaybeFunctionType();
153 |           FunctionType supremum =
154 |               alternate.toMaybeFunctionType().supAndInfHelper(other, true);
155 |           alternates.set(functionTypePosition, supremum);
156 |           result = null;
157 |           return this;
158 |         }
159 | 
160 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
161 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
162 |         // one another.
163 |         int currentIndex = 0;
164 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
165 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
166 |           boolean removeCurrent = false;
167 |           JSType current = it.next();
168 | 
169 |           // Unknown and NoResolved types may just be names that haven't
170 |           // been resolved yet. So keep these in the union, and just use
171 |           // equality checking for simple de-duping.
172 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
173 |               current.isUnknownType() ||
174 |               alternate.isNoResolvedType() ||
175 |               current.isNoResolvedType() ||
176 |               alternate.hasAnyTemplateTypes() ||
177 |               current.hasAnyTemplateTypes()) {
178 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
179 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
180 |               return this;
181 |             }
182 |           } else {
183 | 
184 |             // Because "Foo" and "Foo.<?>" are roughly equivalent
185 |             // templatized types, special care is needed when building the
186 |             // union. For example:
187 |             //   Object is consider a subtype of Object.<string>
188 |             // but we want to leave "Object" not "Object.<string>" when
189 |             // building the subtype.
190 |             //
191 | 
192 |             if (alternate.isTemplatizedType() || current.isTemplatizedType()) {
193 |               // Cases:
194 |               // 1) alternate:Array.<string> and current:Object ==> Object
195 |               // 2) alternate:Array.<string> and current:Array ==> Array
196 |               // 3) alternate:Object.<string> and
197 |               //    current:Array ==> Array|Object.<string>
198 |               // 4) alternate:Object and current:Array.<string> ==> Object
199 |               // 5) alternate:Array and current:Array.<string> ==> Array
200 |               // 6) alternate:Array and
201 |               //    current:Object.<string> ==> Array|Object.<string>
202 |               // 7) alternate:Array.<string> and
203 |               //    current:Array.<number> ==> Array.<?>
204 |               // 8) alternate:Array.<string> and
205 |               //    current:Array.<string> ==> Array.<string>
206 |               // 9) alternate:Array.<string> and
207 |               //    current:Object.<string> ==> Object.<string>|Array.<string>
208 | 
209 |               if (!current.isTemplatizedType()) {
210 |                 if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
211 |                   // case 1, 2
212 |                   return this;
213 |                 }
214 |                 // case 3: leave current, add alternate
215 |               } else if (!alternate.isTemplatizedType()) {
216 |                 if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
217 |                   // case 4, 5
218 |                   removeCurrent = true;
219 |                 }
220 |                 // case 6: leave current, add alternate
221 |               } else {
222 |                 Preconditions.checkState(current.isTemplatizedType()
223 |                     && alternate.isTemplatizedType());
224 |                 TemplatizedType templatizedAlternate = alternate.toMaybeTemplatizedType();
225 |                 TemplatizedType templatizedCurrent = current.toMaybeTemplatizedType();
226 | 
227 |                 if (templatizedCurrent.wrapsSameRawType(templatizedAlternate)) {
228 |                   if (alternate.getTemplateTypeMap().checkEquivalenceHelper(
229 |                       current.getTemplateTypeMap(),
230 |                       EquivalenceMethod.IDENTITY)) {
231 |                     // case 8
232 |                     return this;
233 |                   } else {
234 |                     // TODO(johnlenz): should we leave both types?
235 |                     // case 7: add a merged alternate
236 |                     // We currently merge to the templatized types to "unknown"
237 |                     // which is equivalent to the raw type.
238 |                     JSType merged = templatizedCurrent
239 |                         .getReferencedObjTypeInternal();
240 |                     return addAlternate(merged);
241 |                   }
242 |                 }
243 |                 // case 9: leave current, add alternate
244 |               }
245 |               // Otherwise leave both templatized types.
246 |             } else if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
247 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
248 |               return this;
249 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
250 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
251 |               removeCurrent = true;
252 |             }
253 |           }
254 | 
255 |           if (removeCurrent) {
256 |             it.remove();
257 | 
258 |             if (currentIndex == functionTypePosition) {
259 |               functionTypePosition = -1;
260 |             } else if (currentIndex < functionTypePosition) {
261 |               functionTypePosition--;
262 |               currentIndex--;
263 |             }
264 |           }
265 |           currentIndex++;
266 |         }
267 | 
268 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType()) {
269 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
270 |           Preconditions.checkState(functionTypePosition == -1);
271 |           functionTypePosition = alternates.size();
272 |         }
273 | 
274 |         alternates.add(alternate);
275 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
276 |       }
277 |     } else {
278 |       result = null;
279 |     }
280 |     return this;
281 |   }

===== M0189 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.hoistExterns(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1424-1460
Documentation:
/**
   * Hoists inputs with the @externs annotation into the externs list.
   */
Method code:
1424 |   private void hoistExterns(Node externsRoot) {
1425 |     boolean staleInputs = false;
1426 |     for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1427 |       if (options.dependencyOptions.needsManagement()) {
1428 |         // If we're doing scanning dependency info anyway, use that
1429 |         // information to skip sources that obviously aren't externs.
1430 |         if (!input.getProvides().isEmpty() || !input.getRequires().isEmpty()) {
1431 |           continue;
1432 |         }
1433 |       }
1434 | 
1435 |       Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1436 | 
1437 |       // Inputs can have a null AST on a parse error.
1438 |       if (n == null) {
1439 |         continue;
1440 |       }
1441 | 
1442 |       JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1443 |       if (info != null && info.isExterns()) {
1444 |         // If the input file is explicitly marked as an externs file, then
1445 |         // assume the programmer made a mistake and throw it into
1446 |         // the externs pile anyways.
1447 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1448 |         input.setIsExtern(true);
1449 | 
1450 |         input.getModule().remove(input);
1451 | 
1452 |         externs.add(input);
1453 |         staleInputs = true;
1454 |       }
1455 |     }
1456 | 
1457 |     if (staleInputs) {
1458 |       repartitionInputs();
1459 |     }
1460 |   }

Method code:
405 |   @Override
406 |   public Iterable<ObjectType> getCtorExtendedInterfaces() {
407 |     return isFunctionPrototypeType()

===== M0197 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.reconcileOptionsWithGuards()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 313-352
Documentation:
/**
   * When the CompilerOptions and its WarningsGuard overlap, reconcile
   * any discrepencies.
   */
Method code:
313 |   protected void reconcileOptionsWithGuards() {
314 |     // DiagnosticGroups override the plain checkTypes option.
315 |     if (options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
316 |       options.checkTypes = true;
317 |     } else if (options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
318 |       options.checkTypes = false;
319 |     } else if (!options.checkTypes) {
320 |       // If DiagnosticGroups did not override the plain checkTypes
321 |       // option, and checkTypes is enabled, then turn off the
322 |       // parser type warnings.
323 |       options.setWarningLevel(
324 |           DiagnosticGroup.forType(
325 |               RhinoErrorReporter.TYPE_PARSE_ERROR),
326 |           CheckLevel.OFF);
327 |     }
328 | 
329 |     if (options.checkGlobalThisLevel.isOn() &&
330 |         !options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS)) {
331 |       options.setWarningLevel(
332 |           DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS,
333 |           options.checkGlobalThisLevel);
334 |     }
335 | 
336 |     if (options.getLanguageIn() == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT5_STRICT) {
337 |       options.setWarningLevel(
338 |           DiagnosticGroups.ES5_STRICT,
339 |           CheckLevel.ERROR);
340 |     }
341 | 
342 |     // All passes must run the variable check. This synthesizes
343 |     // variables later so that the compiler doesn't crash. It also
344 |     // checks the externs file for validity. If you don't want to warn
345 |     // about missing variable declarations, we shut that specific
346 |     // error off.
347 |     if (!options.checkSymbols &&
348 |         !options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES)) {
349 |       options.setWarningLevel(
350 |           DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES, CheckLevel.OFF);
351 |     }
352 |   }

/**
   * Concatenates two ImmutableList instances. If either input is empty, the
   * other is returned; otherwise, a new ImmutableList instance is created that
   * contains the contents of both arguments.
   */
Method code:
307 |   private <T> ImmutableList<T> concatImmutableLists(
308 |     ImmutableList<T> first, ImmutableList<T> second) {
309 |     if (first.isEmpty()) {
310 |       return second;
311 |     }
312 |     if (second.isEmpty()) {
313 |       return first;
314 |     }
315 |     ImmutableList.Builder<T> builder = ImmutableList.builder();
316 |     builder.addAll(first);
317 |     builder.addAll(second);
318 |     return builder.build();
319 |   }

179 |                                           node.getClass() +
180 |                                           " type: " +
181 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
182 |         }
183 |       case Token.BREAK:
184 |         return processBreakStatement((BreakStatement) node);
185 |       case Token.CALL:
186 |         return processFunctionCall((FunctionCall) node);
187 |       case Token.CASE:
188 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
189 |         return processSwitchCase((SwitchCase) node);
190 |       case Token.CATCH:
191 |         return processCatchClause((CatchClause) node);
192 |       case Token.COLON:
193 |         return processObjectProperty((ObjectProperty) node);
194 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
195 |         return processContinueStatement((ContinueStatement) node);
196 |       case Token.DO:
197 |         return processDoLoop((DoLoop) node);
198 |       case Token.EMPTY:
199 |         return (node instanceof EmptyExpression) ?
200 |             processEmptyExpression((EmptyExpression) node) :
201 |             processEmptyStatement((EmptyStatement) node);
202 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
203 |       case Token.EXPR_VOID:

253 |       case Token.REGEXP:

259 |       case Token.STRING:
260 |         return processStringLiteral((StringLiteral) node);
261 |       case Token.SWITCH:
262 |         return processSwitchStatement((SwitchStatement) node);
263 |       case Token.THROW:
264 |         return processThrowStatement((ThrowStatement) node);
265 |       case Token.TRY:
266 |         return processTryStatement((TryStatement) node);
267 |       case Token.CONST:

269 |         if (node instanceof VariableDeclaration) {
270 |           return processVariableDeclaration((VariableDeclaration) node);
271 |         } else  if (node instanceof VariableInitializer) {
272 |           return processVariableInitializer((VariableInitializer) node);
273 |         } else {
274 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
275 |                                           node.getClass() +
276 |                                           " type: " +
277 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
278 |         }
279 |       case Token.WHILE:
280 |         return processWhileLoop((WhileLoop) node);
281 |       case Token.WITH:
282 |         return processWithStatement((WithStatement) node);
283 |     }
284 |     return processIllegalToken(node);
285 |   }

/**
   * Returns true if the operator is associative.
   * e.g. (a * b) * c = a * (b * c)
   * Note: "+" is not associative because it is also the concatenation
   * for strings. e.g. "a" + (1 + 2) is not "a" + 1 + 2
   */
Method code:
1482 |   static boolean isAssociative(int type) {

1488 |       case Token.BITXOR:
1489 |       case Token.BITAND:

1491 |       default:
1492 |         return false;
1493 |     }
1494 |   }

     * This may start a new line if the current line is longer than the line

Method code:
490 |     @Override
491 |     void maybeCutLine() {
492 |       if (lineLength > lineLengthThreshold) {
493 |         // Use the preferred position provided it will break the line.
494 |         if (preferredBreakPosition > lineStartPosition &&
495 |             preferredBreakPosition < lineStartPosition + lineLength) {
496 |           int position = preferredBreakPosition;

499 |           reportLineCut(lineIndex, position - lineStartPosition, true);

502 |           lineStartPosition = position + 1;
503 |         } else {
504 |           startNewLine();
505 |         }

===== M0224 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1229-1231
Documentation:
/**
   * Checks whether {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that}.<p>
   *
   * Subtyping rules:
   * <ul>
   * <li>(unknown) &mdash; every type is a subtype of the Unknown type.</li>
   * <li>(no) &mdash; the No type is a subtype of every type.</li>
   * <li>(no-object) &mdash; the NoObject type is a subtype of every object
   * type (i.e. subtypes of the Object type).</li>
   * <li>(ref) &mdash; a type is a subtype of itself.</li>
   * <li>(union-l) &mdash; A union type is a subtype of a type U if all the
   * union type's constituents are a subtype of U. Formally<br>
   * {@code (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>) &lt;: U} if and only
   * {@code T<sub>k</sub> &lt;: U} for all {@code k &isin; 1..n}.</li>
   * <li>(union-r) &mdash; A type U is a subtype of a union type if it is a
   * subtype of one of the union type's constituents. Formally<br>
   * {@code U &lt;: (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>)} if and only
   * if {@code U &lt;: T<sub>k</sub>} for some index {@code k}.</li>
   * <li>(objects) &mdash; an Object {@code O<sub>1</sub>} is a subtype
   * of an object {@code O<sub>2</sub>} if it has more properties
   * than {@code O<sub>2</sub>} and all common properties are
   * pairwise subtypes.</li>
   * </ul>
   *
   * @return {@code this &lt;: that}
   */
Method code:
1229 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
1230 |     return isSubtypeHelper(this, that);
1231 |   }

/**
   * Create and start a tracer.
   * Both type and comment may be null. See class comment for usage.
   *
   * @param type The type for totaling
   * @param comment Comment about this tracer
   */
Method code:
235 |   Tracer(@Nullable String type, @Nullable String comment) {
236 |     this.type = type;
237 |     this.comment = comment == null ? "" : comment;
238 |     startTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
239 |     startThread = Thread.currentThread();
240 |     if (!extraTracingStatistics.isEmpty()) {
241 |       int size = extraTracingStatistics.size();
242 |       extraTracingValues = new long[size];
243 |       int i = 0;
244 |       for (TracingStatistic tracingStatistic : extraTracingStatistics) {
245 |         extraTracingValues[i] = tracingStatistic.start(startThread);
246 |         i++;
247 |       }
248 |     }
249 | 
250 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
251 | 
252 |     // Do nothing if the current thread trace wasn't initialized.
253 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
254 |       return;
255 |     }
256 | 
257 |     // Check if we are creating too many Tracers.
258 |     if (trace.events.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
259 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
260 |                   "Giant thread trace. Too many Tracers created. "
261 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
262 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
263 |       trace.truncateEvents();
264 |     }
265 | 
266 |     // Check if we forgot to close the Tracers.
267 |     if (trace.outstandingEvents.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
268 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
269 |                   "Too many outstanding Tracers. Tracer.stop() is missing "
270 |                     + "or Tracer.stop() is not wrapped in a "
271 |                     + "try/finally block. "
272 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
273 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
274 |       trace.truncateOutstandingEvents();
275 |     }
276 | 
277 |     trace.startEvent(this);
278 |   }

===== M0237 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isImmutableValue(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 491-515
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if this is an immutable value.
   */
Method code:
491 |   static boolean isImmutableValue(Node n) {
492 |     switch (n.getType()) {
493 |       case Token.STRING:
494 |       case Token.NUMBER:
495 |       case Token.NULL:
496 |       case Token.TRUE:
497 |       case Token.FALSE:
498 |         return true;
499 |       case Token.CAST:
500 |       case Token.NOT:
501 |         return isImmutableValue(n.getFirstChild());
502 |       case Token.VOID:
503 |       case Token.NEG:
504 |         return isImmutableValue(n.getFirstChild());
505 |       case Token.NAME:
506 |         String name = n.getString();
507 |         // We assume here that programs don't change the value of the keyword
508 |         // undefined to something other than the value undefined.
509 |         return "undefined".equals(name)
510 |             || "Infinity".equals(name)
511 |             || "NaN".equals(name);
512 |     }
513 | 
514 |     return false;
515 |   }

===== M0245 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 515-541
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
515 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
516 |     int type = n.getType();
517 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
518 |       inputId = n.getInputId();
519 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
520 |     }
521 | 
522 |     curNode = n;
523 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) {
524 |       return;
525 |     }
526 | 
527 |     if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
528 |       traverseFunction(n, parent);
529 |     } else {
530 |       for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
531 |         // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
532 |         // would no longer point to the true next
533 |         Node next = child.getNext();
534 |         traverseBranch(child, n);
535 |         child = next;
536 |       }
537 |     }
538 | 
539 |     curNode = n;
540 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
541 |   }

===== M0246 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.addOp(String,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 209-237
Documentation:

Method code:
209 |   void addOp(String op, boolean binOp) {
210 |     maybeEndStatement();
211 | 
212 |     char first = op.charAt(0);
213 |     char prev = getLastChar();
214 | 
215 |     if ((first == '+' || first == '-') && prev == first) {
216 |       // This is not pretty printing. This is to prevent misparsing of
217 |       // things like "x + ++y" or "x++ + ++y"
218 |       append(" ");
219 |     } else if (Character.isLetter(first) &&
220 |                isWordChar(prev)) {
221 |       // Make sure there is a space after e.g. instanceof , typeof
222 |       append(" ");
223 |     } else if (prev == '-' && first == '>') {
224 |       // Make sure that we don't emit -->
225 |       append(" ");
226 |     }
227 | 
228 |     // Allow formatting around the operator.
229 |     appendOp(op, binOp);
230 | 
231 |     // Line breaking after an operator is always safe. Line breaking before an
232 |     // operator on the other hand is not. We only line break after a bin op
233 |     // because it looks strange.
234 |     if (binOp) {
235 |       maybeCutLine();
236 |     }
237 |   }

Method code:
81 |   private void parse(AbstractCompiler compiler) {
82 |     try {
83 |       logger_.fine("Parsing: " + sourceFile.getName());
84 |       ParserRunner.ParseResult result = ParserRunner.parse(sourceFile, sourceFile.getCode(),
85 |           compiler.getParserConfig(),
86 |           compiler.getDefaultErrorReporter(),
87 |           logger_);
88 |       root = result.ast;
89 |       compiler.setOldParseTree(sourceFile.getName(), result.oldAst);
90 |     } catch (IOException e) {
91 |       compiler.report(
92 |           JSError.make(AbstractCompiler.READ_ERROR, sourceFile.getName()));
93 |     }
94 | 
95 |     if (root == null || compiler.hasHaltingErrors()) {
96 |       // There was a parse error or IOException, so use a dummy block.
97 |       root = IR.script();
98 |     } else {
99 |       compiler.prepareAst(root);
100 |     }
101 | 
102 |     // Set the source name so that the compiler passes can track
103 |     // the source file and module.
104 |     root.setStaticSourceFile(sourceFile);
105 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionParamBuilder.addVarArgs(JSType)

/**
   * Add variable arguments to the end of the parameter list.
   * @return False if this is called after var args are added.
   */
Method code:
94 |   public boolean addVarArgs(JSType type) {
95 |     if (hasVarArgs()) {
96 |       return false;
97 |     }
98 | 
99 |     // There are two types of variable argument functions:
100 |     // 1) Programmer-defined var args
101 |     // 2) Native bottom types that can accept any argument.
102 |     // For the first one, "undefined" is a valid value for all arguments.
103 |     // For the second, we do not want to cast it up to undefined.
104 |     if (!type.isEmptyType()) {
105 |       type = registry.createOptionalType(type);
106 |     }
107 |     newParameter(type).setVarArgs(true);
108 |     return true;
109 |   }

===== M0261 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseChangedFunctions(AbstractCompiler,FunctionCallback)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 471-486
Documentation:
/**
   * Traversal for passes that work only on changed functions.
   * Suppose a loopable pass P1 uses this traversal.
   * Then, if a function doesn't change between two runs of P1, it won't look at
   * the function the second time.
   * (We're assuming that P1 runs to a fixpoint, o/w we may miss optimizations.)
   *
   * Most changes are reported with calls to Compiler.reportCodeChange(), which
   * doesn't know which scope changed. We keep track of the current scope by
   * calling Compiler.setScope inside pushScope and popScope.
   * The automatic tracking can be wrong in rare cases when a pass changes scope
   * w/out causing a call to pushScope or popScope. It's very hard to find the
   * places where this happens unless a bug is triggered.
   * Passes that do cross-scope modifications call
   * Compiler.reportChangeToEnclosingScope(Node n).
   */
Method code:
471 |   public static void traverseChangedFunctions(
472 |       AbstractCompiler compiler, FunctionCallback callback) {
473 |     final AbstractCompiler comp = compiler;
474 |     final FunctionCallback cb = callback;
475 |     final Node jsRoot = comp.getJsRoot();
476 |     NodeTraversal t = new NodeTraversal(comp, new AbstractPreOrderCallback() {
477 |         @Override
478 |         public final boolean shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node p) {
479 |           if ((n == jsRoot || n.isFunction()) && comp.hasScopeChanged(n)) {
480 |             cb.visit(comp, n);
481 |           }
482 |           return true;
483 |         }
484 |       });
485 |     t.traverse(jsRoot);
486 |   }

1701 |       // PropListItem lists are immutable and can be shared so there is no
1702 |       // need to clone them here.
1703 |       result.next = null;
1704 |       result.first = null;
1705 |       result.last = null;
1706 |       result.parent = null;
1707 |     } catch (CloneNotSupportedException e) {
1708 |       throw new RuntimeException(e.getMessage());
1709 |     }
1710 |     return result;
1711 |   }

===== M0271 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtypeHelper(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1237-1297
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
1237 |   static boolean isSubtypeHelper(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
1238 |     // unknown
1239 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
1240 |       return true;
1241 |     }
1242 |     // all type
1243 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
1244 |       return true;
1245 |     }
1246 |     // equality
1247 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
1248 |       return true;
1249 |     }
1250 |     // unions
1251 |     if (thatType.isUnionType()) {
1252 |       UnionType union = thatType.toMaybeUnionType();
1253 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
1254 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
1255 |           return true;
1256 |         }
1257 |       }
1258 |       return false;
1259 |     }
1260 | 
1261 |     // TemplateTypeMaps. This check only returns false if the TemplateTypeMaps
1262 |     // are not equivalent.
1263 |     TemplateTypeMap thisTypeParams = thisType.getTemplateTypeMap();
1264 |     TemplateTypeMap thatTypeParams = thatType.getTemplateTypeMap();
1265 |     boolean templateMatch = true;
1266 |     if (isExemptFromTemplateTypeInvariance(thatType)) {
1267 |       // Array and Object are exempt from template type invariance; their
1268 |       // template types maps are considered a match only if the ObjectElementKey
1269 |       // values are subtypes/supertypes of one another.
1270 |       TemplateType key = thisType.registry.getObjectElementKey();
1271 |       JSType thisElement = thisTypeParams.getTemplateType(key);
1272 |       JSType thatElement = thatTypeParams.getTemplateType(key);
1273 | 
1274 |       templateMatch = thisElement.isSubtype(thatElement)
1275 |           || thatElement.isSubtype(thisElement);
1276 |     } else {
1277 |       templateMatch = thisTypeParams.checkEquivalenceHelper(
1278 |           thatTypeParams, EquivalenceMethod.INVARIANT);
1279 |     }
1280 |     if (!templateMatch) {
1281 |       return false;
1282 |     }
1283 | 
1284 |     // Templatized types. The above check guarantees TemplateTypeMap
1285 |     // equivalence; check if the base type is a subtype.
1286 |     if (thisType.isTemplatizedType()) {
1287 |       return thisType.toMaybeTemplatizedType().getReferencedType().isSubtype(
1288 |               thatType);
1289 |     }
1290 | 
1291 |     // proxy types
1292 |     if (thatType instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
1293 |       return thisType.isSubtype(
1294 |           ((ProxyObjectType) thatType).getReferencedTypeInternal());
1295 |     }
1296 |     return false;
1297 |   }

===== M0280 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.checkEquivalenceHelper(JSType,EquivalenceMethod)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 571-639
Documentation:
/**
   * An equivalence visitor.
   */
Method code:
571 |   boolean checkEquivalenceHelper(JSType that, EquivalenceMethod eqMethod) {
572 |     if (this == that) {
573 |       return true;
574 |     }
575 | 
576 |     boolean thisUnknown = isUnknownType();
577 |     boolean thatUnknown = that.isUnknownType();
578 |     if (thisUnknown || thatUnknown) {
579 |       if (eqMethod == EquivalenceMethod.INVARIANT) {
580 |         // If we're checking for invariance, the unknown type is invariant
581 |         // with everyone.
582 |         return true;
583 |       } else if (eqMethod == EquivalenceMethod.DATA_FLOW) {
584 |         // If we're checking data flow, then two types are the same if they're
585 |         // both unknown.
586 |         return thisUnknown && thatUnknown;
587 |       } else if (thisUnknown && thatUnknown &&
588 |           (isNominalType() ^ that.isNominalType())) {
589 |         // If they're both unknown, but one is a nominal type and the other
590 |         // is not, then we should fail out immediately. This ensures that
591 |         // we won't unbox the unknowns further down.
592 |         return false;
593 |       }
594 |     }
595 | 
596 |     if (isUnionType() && that.isUnionType()) {
597 |       return toMaybeUnionType().checkUnionEquivalenceHelper(
598 |           that.toMaybeUnionType(), eqMethod);
599 |     }
600 | 
601 |     if (isFunctionType() && that.isFunctionType()) {
602 |       return toMaybeFunctionType().checkFunctionEquivalenceHelper(
603 |           that.toMaybeFunctionType(), eqMethod);
604 |     }
605 | 
606 |     if (isRecordType() && that.isRecordType()) {
607 |       return toMaybeRecordType().checkRecordEquivalenceHelper(
608 |           that.toMaybeRecordType(), eqMethod);
609 |     }
610 | 
611 |     if (!getTemplateTypeMap().checkEquivalenceHelper(
612 |         that.getTemplateTypeMap(), eqMethod)) {
613 |       return false;
614 |     }
615 | 
616 |     if (isNominalType() && that.isNominalType()) {
617 |       return toObjectType().getReferenceName().equals(
618 |           that.toObjectType().getReferenceName());
619 |     }
620 | 
621 |     // Unbox other proxies.
622 |     if (this instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
623 |       return ((ProxyObjectType) this)
624 |           .getReferencedTypeInternal().checkEquivalenceHelper(
625 |               that, eqMethod);
626 |     }
627 | 
628 |     if (that instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
629 |       return checkEquivalenceHelper(
630 |           ((ProxyObjectType) that).getReferencedTypeInternal(),
631 |           eqMethod);
632 |     }
633 | 
634 |     // Relies on the fact that for the base {@link JSType}, only one
635 |     // instance of each sub-type will ever be created in a given registry, so
636 |     // there is no need to verify members. If the object pointers are not
637 |     // identical, then the type member must be different.
638 |     return this == that;
639 |   }

3365 |           public void visit(Node n) {
3366 |             if (n.isFunction() && mtoc.containsKey(n)) {
3367 |               Node clone = mtoc.get(n);
3368 |               if (n.getChangeTime() > clone.getChangeTime()) {
3369 |                 Preconditions.checkState(!n.isEquivalentToShallow(clone));
3370 |               } else if (checkUnchanged) {
3371 |                 Preconditions.checkState(n.isEquivalentToShallow(clone));
3372 |               }
3373 |             }
3374 |           }

===== M0321 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ErrorFunctionType.ErrorFunctionType(JSTypeRegistry,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ErrorFunctionType.java
Original range: 50-74
Documentation:

Method code:
50 |   ErrorFunctionType(JSTypeRegistry registry, String name) {
51 |     super(
52 |         registry, name, null,
53 |         registry.createArrowType(
54 |             registry.createOptionalParameters(
55 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE),
56 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE),
57 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE)),
58 |             null),
59 |         null, null, true, true);
60 | 
61 |     // NOTE(nicksantos): Errors have the weird behavior in that they can
62 |     // be called as functions, and they will return instances of themselves.
63 |     // Error('x') instanceof Error => true
64 |     //
65 |     // In user-defined types, we would deal with this case by creating
66 |     // a NamedType with the name "Error" and then resolve it later.
67 |     //
68 |     // For native types, we don't really want the native types to
69 |     // depend on type-resolution. So we just set the return type manually
70 |     // at the end of construction.
71 |     //
72 |     // There's similar logic in JSTypeRegistry for Array and RegExp.
73 |     getInternalArrowType().returnType = getInstanceType();
74 |   }

===== M0324 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.isUnknownType()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ObjectType.java
Original range: 552-572
Documentation:
/**
   * We treat this as the unknown type if any of its implicit prototype
   * properties is unknown.
   */
Method code:
552 |   @Override
553 |   public boolean isUnknownType() {
554 |     // If the object is unknown now, check the supertype again,
555 |     // because it might have been resolved since the last check.
556 |     if (unknown) {
557 |       ObjectType implicitProto = getImplicitPrototype();
558 |       if (implicitProto == null ||
559 |           implicitProto.isNativeObjectType()) {
560 |         unknown = false;
561 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
562 |           if (interfaceType.isUnknownType()) {
563 |             unknown = true;
564 |             break;
565 |           }
566 |         }
567 |       } else {
568 |         unknown = implicitProto.isUnknownType();
569 |       }
570 |     }
571 |     return unknown;
572 |   }

===== M0328 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isLatin(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2479-2488
Documentation:
/** Test if all characters in the string are in the Basic Latin (aka ASCII)
   * character set - that they have UTF-16 values equal to or below 0x7f.
   * This check can find which identifiers with Unicode characters need to be
   * escaped in order to allow resulting files to be processed by non-Unicode
   * aware UNIX tools and editors.
   * *
   * See http://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Latin_characters_in_Unicode
   * for more on Basic Latin.
   *
   * @param s The string to be checked for ASCII-goodness.
   *
   * @return True if all characters in the string are in Basic Latin set.
   */
Method code:
2479 |   static boolean isLatin(String s) {
2480 |     int len = s.length();
2481 |     for (int index = 0; index < len; index++) {
2482 |       char c = s.charAt(index);
2483 |       if (c > LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN) {
2484 |         return false;
2485 |       }
2486 |     }
2487 |     return true;
2488 |   }

===== M0337 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ExploitAssigns.isCollapsibleValue(Node,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ExploitAssigns.java
Original range: 76-91
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines whether we know enough about the given value to be able
   * to collapse it into subsequent expressions.
   *
   * For example, we can collapse booleans and variable names:
   * <code>
   * x = 3; y = x; // y = x = 3;
   * a = true; b = true; // b = a = true;
   * <code>
   * But we won't try to collapse complex expressions.
   *
   * @param value The value node.
   * @param isLValue Whether it's on the left-hand side of an expr.
   */
Method code:
76 |   private static boolean isCollapsibleValue(Node value, boolean isLValue) {
77 |     switch (value.getType()) {
78 |       case Token.GETPROP:
79 |         // Do not collapse GETPROPs on arbitrary objects, because
80 |         // they may be implemented setter functions, and oftentimes
81 |         // setter functions fail on native objects. This is OK for "THIS"
82 |         // objects, because we assume that they are non-native.
83 |         return !isLValue || value.getFirstChild().isThis();
84 | 
85 |       case Token.NAME:
86 |         return true;
87 | 
88 |       default:
89 |         return NodeUtil.isImmutableValue(value);
90 |     }
91 |   }

59 |   @Override
60 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
61 |     compiler.addChangeHandler(handler);
62 |     beginTraversal();
63 |     NodeTraversal.traverseChangedFunctions(compiler, new FunctionCallback() {
64 |         @Override
65 |         public void visit(AbstractCompiler compiler, Node root) {

74 |       });
75 |     endTraversal();

77 |   }

===== M0354 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.identifierEscape(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 1170-1190
Documentation:

Method code:
1170 |   static String identifierEscape(String s) {
1171 |     // First check if escaping is needed at all -- in most cases it isn't.
1172 |     if (NodeUtil.isLatin(s)) {
1173 |       return s;
1174 |     }
1175 | 
1176 |     // Now going through the string to escape non-Latin characters if needed.
1177 |     StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
1178 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
1179 |       char c = s.charAt(i);
1180 |       // Identifiers should always go to Latin1/ ASCII characters because
1181 |       // different browser's rules for valid identifier characters are
1182 |       // crazy.
1183 |       if (c > 0x1F && c < 0x7F) {
1184 |         sb.append(c);
1185 |       } else {
1186 |         appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
1187 |       }
1188 |     }
1189 |     return sb.toString();
1190 |   }

/**
   * Downcasts this to a FunctionType, or returns null if this is not
   * a function.
   *
   * For the purposes of this function, we define a MaybeFunctionType as any
   * type in the sub-lattice
   * { x | LEAST_FUNCTION_TYPE <= x <= GREATEST_FUNCTION_TYPE }
   * This definition excludes bottom types like NoType and NoObjectType.
   *
   * This definition is somewhat arbitrary and axiomatic, but this is the
   * definition that makes the most sense for the most callers.
   */

===== M0362 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.verifyScopeChanges(Map<Node, Node>,Node,boolean,AbstractCompiler)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 3343-3377
Documentation:
/** Checks that the scope roots marked as changed have indeed changed */
Method code:
3343 |   public static void verifyScopeChanges(Map<Node, Node> map,
3344 |       Node main, boolean verifyUnchangedNodes,
3345 |       AbstractCompiler compiler) {
3346 |     // compiler is passed only to call compiler.toSource during debugging to see
3347 |     // mismatches in scopes
3348 | 
3349 |     // If verifyUnchangedNodes is false, we are comparing the initial AST to the
3350 |     // final AST. Don't check unmarked nodes b/c they may have been changed by
3351 |     // non-loopable passes.
3352 |     // If verifyUnchangedNodes is true, we are comparing the ASTs before & after
3353 |     // a pass. Check all scope roots.
3354 |     final Map<Node, Node> mtoc = map;
3355 |     final boolean checkUnchanged = verifyUnchangedNodes;
3356 |     Node clone = mtoc.get(main);
3357 |     if (main.getChangeTime() > clone.getChangeTime()) {
3358 |       Preconditions.checkState(!main.isEquivalentToShallow(clone));
3359 |     } else if (checkUnchanged) {
3360 |       Preconditions.checkState(main.isEquivalentToShallow(clone));
3361 |     }
3362 |     visitPreOrder(main,
3363 |         new Visitor() {
3364 |           @Override
3365 |           public void visit(Node n) {
3366 |             if (n.isFunction() && mtoc.containsKey(n)) {
3367 |               Node clone = mtoc.get(n);
3368 |               if (n.getChangeTime() > clone.getChangeTime()) {
3369 |                 Preconditions.checkState(!n.isEquivalentToShallow(clone));
3370 |               } else if (checkUnchanged) {
3371 |                 Preconditions.checkState(n.isEquivalentToShallow(clone));
3372 |               }
3373 |             }
3374 |           }
3375 |         },
3376 |         Predicates.<Node>alwaysTrue());
3377 |   }

524 |       case Token.VAR:

===== M0381 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.isEquivalentTo(Node,boolean,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1512-1563
Documentation:
/**
   * @param compareJsType Whether to compare the JSTypes of the nodes.
   * @param recur Whether to compare the children of the current node, if
   *    not only the the count of the children are compared.
   * @param shallow If true, the method doesn't recur into inner functions.
   * @return Whether this node is equivalent semantically to the provided node.
   */
Method code:
1512 |   boolean isEquivalentTo(
1513 |       Node node, boolean compareJsType, boolean recur, boolean shallow) {
1514 |     if (type != node.getType()
1515 |         || getChildCount() != node.getChildCount()
1516 |         || this.getClass() != node.getClass()) {
1517 |       return false;
1518 |     }
1519 | 
1520 |     if (compareJsType && !JSType.isEquivalent(jsType, node.getJSType())) {
1521 |       return false;
1522 |     }
1523 | 
1524 |     if (type == Token.INC || type == Token.DEC) {
1525 |       int post1 = this.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1526 |       int post2 = node.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1527 |       if (post1 != post2) {
1528 |         return false;
1529 |       }
1530 |     } else if (type == Token.STRING || type == Token.STRING_KEY) {
1531 |       if (type == Token.STRING_KEY) {
1532 |         int quoted1 = this.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1533 |         int quoted2 = node.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1534 |         if (quoted1 != quoted2) {
1535 |           return false;
1536 |         }
1537 |       }
1538 | 
1539 |       int slashV1 = this.getIntProp(SLASH_V);
1540 |       int slashV2 = node.getIntProp(SLASH_V);
1541 |       if (slashV1 != slashV2) {
1542 |         return false;
1543 |       }
1544 |     } else if (type == Token.CALL) {
1545 |       if (this.getBooleanProp(FREE_CALL) != node.getBooleanProp(FREE_CALL)) {
1546 |         return false;
1547 |       }
1548 |     }
1549 | 
1550 |     if (recur) {
1551 |       Node n, n2;
1552 |       for (n = first, n2 = node.first;
1553 |            n != null;
1554 |            n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1555 |         if (!n.isEquivalentTo(
1556 |             n2, compareJsType, !(shallow && n.isFunction()), shallow)) {
1557 |           return false;
1558 |         }
1559 |       }
1560 |     }
1561 | 
1562 |     return true;
1563 |   }

Original range: 80-97

Method code:
80 |     @Override
81 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
82 |       Node currentNode = n, newNode;
83 |       boolean codeChanged = false;
84 |       do {
85 |         codeChanged = false;
86 |         for (AbstractPeepholeOptimization optim : peepholeOptimizations) {

===== M0396 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.precedence(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1190-1197
Documentation:
/*
   *  0 comma ,
   *  1 assignment = += -= *= /= %= <<= >>= >>>= &= ^= |=
   *  2 conditional ?:
   *  3 logical-or ||
   *  4 logical-and &&
   *  5 bitwise-or |
   *  6 bitwise-xor ^
   *  7 bitwise-and &
   *  8 equality == !=
   *  9 relational < <= > >=
   * 10 bitwise shift << >> >>>
   * 11 addition/subtraction + -
   * 12 multiply/divide * / %
   * 13 negation/increment ! ~ - ++ --
   * 14 call, member () [] .
   */
Method code:
1190 |   static int precedence(int type) {
1191 |     int precedence = precedenceWithDefault(type);
1192 |     if (precedence != -1) {
1193 |       return precedence;
1194 |     }
1195 |     throw new Error("Unknown precedence for " +
1196 |         Token.name(type) + " (type " + type + ")");
1197 |   }

/**
   * @param item The item to inspect
   * @param propType The property to look for
   * @return The replacement list if the property was removed, or
   *   'item' otherwise.
   */
Method code:
794 |   private PropListItem removeProp(PropListItem item, int propType) {
795 |     if (item == null) {
796 |       return null;
797 |     } else if (item.getType() == propType) {
798 |       return item.getNext();
799 |     } else {
800 |       PropListItem result = removeProp(item.getNext(), propType);
801 |       if (result != item.getNext()) {
802 |         return item.chain(result);
803 |       } else {
804 |         return item;
805 |       }
806 |     }
807 |   }

===== M0429 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.unrollBinaryOperator(Node,int,String,Context,Context,int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 766-782
Documentation:
/**
   * We could use addList recursively here, but sometimes we produce
   * very deeply nested operators and run out of stack space, so we
   * just unroll the recursion when possible.
   *
   * We assume nodes are left-recursive.
   */
Method code:
766 |   private void unrollBinaryOperator(
767 |       Node n, int op, String opStr, Context context,
768 |       Context rhsContext, int leftPrecedence, int rightPrecedence) {
769 |     Node firstNonOperator = n.getFirstChild();
770 |     while (firstNonOperator.getType() == op) {
771 |       firstNonOperator = firstNonOperator.getFirstChild();
772 |     }
773 | 
774 |     addExpr(firstNonOperator, leftPrecedence, context);
775 | 
776 |     Node current = firstNonOperator;
777 |     do {
778 |       current = current.getParent();
779 |       cc.addOp(opStr, true);
780 |       addExpr(current.getFirstChild().getNext(), rightPrecedence, rhsContext);
781 |     } while (current != n);
782 |   }

/**
   * Hoists inputs with the @nocompiler annotation out of the inputs.
   */
Method code:
1465 |   private void hoistNoCompileFiles() {
1466 |     boolean staleInputs = false;
1467 |     for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1468 |       Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1469 | 
1470 |       // Inputs can have a null AST on a parse error.
1471 |       if (n == null) {
1472 |         continue;
1473 |       }
1474 | 
1475 |       JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1476 |       if (info != null && info.isNoCompile()) {
1477 |         input.getModule().remove(input);
1478 |         staleInputs = true;
1479 |       }
1480 |     }
1481 | 
1482 |     if (staleInputs) {
1483 |       repartitionInputs();
1484 |     }
1485 |   }

===== M0437 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1442-1461
Documentation:
/**
   * Compare this node to node2 recursively and return the first pair of nodes
   * that differs doing a preorder depth-first traversal. Package private for
   * testing. Returns null if the nodes are equivalent.
   */
Method code:
1442 |   NodeMismatch checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node node2) {
1443 |     if (!isEquivalentTo(node2, false, false, false)) {
1444 |       return new NodeMismatch(this, node2);
1445 |     }
1446 | 
1447 |     NodeMismatch res = null;
1448 |     Node n, n2;
1449 |     for (n = first, n2 = node2.first;
1450 |          res == null && n != null;
1451 |          n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1452 |       if (node2 == null) {
1453 |         throw new IllegalStateException();
1454 |       }
1455 |       res = n.checkTreeEqualsImpl(n2);
1456 |       if (res != null) {
1457 |         return res;
1458 |       }
1459 |     }
1460 |     return res;
1461 |   }

Original range: 464-484

Method code:
464 |     @Override
465 |     void maybeLineBreak() {
466 |       if (lineBreak) {
467 |         if (sawFunction) {
468 |           startNewLine();
469 |           sawFunction = false;
470 |         }
471 |       }
472 | 
473 |       // Since we are at a legal line break, can we upgrade the
474 |       // preferred break position?  We prefer to break after a
475 |       // semicolon rather than before it.
476 |       int len = code.length();
477 |       if (preferredBreakPosition == len - 1) {
478 |         char ch = code.charAt(len - 1);
479 |         if (ch == ';') {
480 |           preferredBreakPosition = len;
481 |         }
482 |       }
483 |       maybeCutLine();
484 |     }

===== M0445 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.toString(StringBuilder,boolean,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 944-1003
Documentation:

Method code:
944 |   private void toString(
945 |       StringBuilder sb,
946 |       boolean printSource,
947 |       boolean printAnnotations,
948 |       boolean printType) {
949 |     sb.append(Token.name(type));
950 |     if (this instanceof StringNode) {
951 |       sb.append(' ');
952 |       sb.append(getString());
953 |     } else if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
954 |       sb.append(' ');
955 |       // In the case of JsDoc trees, the first child is often not a string
956 |       // which causes exceptions to be thrown when calling toString or
957 |       // toStringTree.
958 |       if (first == null || first.getType() != Token.NAME) {
959 |         sb.append("<invalid>");
960 |       } else {
961 |         sb.append(first.getString());
962 |       }
963 |     } else if (type == Token.NUMBER) {
964 |       sb.append(' ');
965 |       sb.append(getDouble());
966 |     }
967 |     if (printSource) {
968 |       int lineno = getLineno();
969 |       if (lineno != -1) {
970 |         sb.append(' ');
971 |         sb.append(lineno);
972 |       }
973 |     }
974 | 
975 |     if (printAnnotations) {
976 |       int[] keys = getSortedPropTypes();
977 |       for (int i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
978 |         int type = keys[i];
979 |         PropListItem x = lookupProperty(type);
980 |         sb.append(" [");
981 |         sb.append(propToString(type));
982 |         sb.append(": ");
983 |         String value;
984 |         switch (type) {
985 |           default:
986 |             value = x.toString();
987 |             break;
988 |         }
989 |         sb.append(value);
990 |         sb.append(']');
991 |       }
992 |     }
993 | 
994 |     if (printType) {
995 |       if (jsType != null) {
996 |         String jsTypeString = jsType.toString();
997 |         if (jsTypeString != null) {
998 |           sb.append(" : ");
999 |           sb.append(jsTypeString);
1000 |         }
1001 |       }
1002 |     }
1003 |   }

===== M0456 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.add(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 180-203
Documentation:

Method code:
180 |   void add(String newcode) {
181 |     maybeEndStatement();
182 | 
183 |     if (newcode.length() == 0) {
184 |       return;
185 |     }
186 | 
187 |     char c = newcode.charAt(0);
188 |     if ((isWordChar(c) || c == '\\') &&
189 |         isWordChar(getLastChar())) {
190 |       // need space to separate. This is not pretty printing.
191 |       // For example: "return foo;"
192 |       append(" ");
193 |     } else if (c == '/' && getLastChar() == '/') {
194 |       // Do not allow a forward slash to appear after a DIV.
195 |       // For example,
196 |       // REGEXP DIV REGEXP
197 |       // is valid and should print like
198 |       // / // / /
199 |       append(" ");
200 |     }
201 | 
202 |     append(newcode);
203 |   }

434 |   private void setSourceInfo(Node irNode, AstNode node) {
435 |     if (irNode.getLineno() == -1) {

444 |     }

===== M0492 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 603-618
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * ECMA-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
603 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
604 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
605 |       Set<String> directives = null;
606 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
607 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
608 |         if (directives == null) {
609 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
610 |         } else {
611 |           directives.add(directive);
612 |         }
613 |       }
614 | 
615 |       if (directives != null) {
616 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
617 |       }
618 |     }

Method code:
1199 |   static int precedenceWithDefault(int type) {
1200 |     switch (type) {
1201 |       case Token.COMMA:  return 0;
1202 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
1203 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
1204 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
1205 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
1206 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
1207 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
1208 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
1209 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
1210 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
1211 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
1212 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
1213 |       case Token.ASSIGN: return 1;
1214 |       case Token.HOOK:   return 2;  // ?: operator
1215 |       case Token.OR:     return 3;
1216 |       case Token.AND:    return 4;
1217 |       case Token.BITOR:  return 5;
1218 |       case Token.BITXOR: return 6;
1219 |       case Token.BITAND: return 7;
1220 |       case Token.EQ:
1221 |       case Token.NE:
1222 |       case Token.SHEQ:
1223 |       case Token.SHNE:   return 8;
1224 |       case Token.LT:
1225 |       case Token.GT:
1226 |       case Token.LE:
1227 |       case Token.GE:
1228 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
1229 |       case Token.IN:     return 9;
1230 |       case Token.LSH:
1231 |       case Token.RSH:
1232 |       case Token.URSH:   return 10;
1233 |       case Token.SUB:
1234 |       case Token.ADD:    return 11;
1235 |       case Token.MUL:
1236 |       case Token.MOD:
1237 |       case Token.DIV:    return 12;
1238 |       case Token.INC:
1239 |       case Token.DEC:
1240 |       case Token.NEW:
1241 |       case Token.DELPROP:
1242 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
1243 |       case Token.VOID:
1244 |       case Token.NOT:
1245 |       case Token.BITNOT:
1246 |       case Token.POS:
1247 |       case Token.NEG:    return 13;
1248 | 
1249 |       case Token.CALL:
1250 |       case Token.GETELEM:
1251 |       case Token.GETPROP:
1252 |       // Data values
1253 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
1254 |       case Token.EMPTY:  // TODO(johnlenz): remove this.
1255 |       case Token.FALSE:
1256 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
1257 |       case Token.NAME:
1258 |       case Token.NULL:
1259 |       case Token.NUMBER:
1260 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
1261 |       case Token.REGEXP:
1262 |       case Token.STRING:
1263 |       case Token.STRING_KEY:

1265 |       case Token.TRUE:
1266 |         return 15;
1267 |       case Token.CAST:
1268 |         return 16;
1269 | 
1270 |       default:
1271 |         // Statements are lower precedence than expressions.
1272 |         return -1;
1273 |     }
1274 |   }

179 |   public static Node transformTree(AstRoot node,

187 |

===== M0513 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.stop(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 407-434
Documentation:
/**
   * Stop the trace.
   * This may only be done once and must be done from the same thread
   * that started it.
   * @param silenceThreshold Traces for time less than silence_threshold
   * ms will be left out of the trace report. A value of -1 indicates
   * that the current ThreadTrace silence_threshold should be used.
   * @return The time that this trace actually ran
   */
Method code:
407 |   long stop(int silenceThreshold) {
408 |     Preconditions.checkState(Thread.currentThread() == startThread);
409 | 
410 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
411 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
412 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
413 |       return 0;
414 |     }
415 | 
416 |     stopTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
417 |     if (extraTracingValues != null) {
418 |       // We use extraTracingValues.length rather than
419 |       // extraTracingStatistics.size() because a new statistic may
420 |       // have been added
421 |       for (int i = 0; i < extraTracingValues.length; i++) {
422 |         long value = extraTracingStatistics.get(i).stop(startThread);
423 |         extraTracingValues[i] = value - extraTracingValues[i];
424 |       }
425 |     }
426 | 
427 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
428 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
429 |       return 0;
430 |     }
431 | 
432 |     trace.endEvent(this, silenceThreshold);
433 |     return stopTimeMs - startTimeMs;
434 |   }
