===== M0520 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 316-363
Documentation:

Method code:
316 |   @Override
317 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
318 |     if (JSType.isSubtypeHelper(this, that)) {
319 |       return true;
320 |     }
321 | 
322 |     // Union types
323 |     if (that.isUnionType()) {
324 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
325 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
326 |       return false;
327 |     }
328 | 
329 |     // record types
330 |     if (that.isRecordType()) {
331 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, that.toMaybeRecordType());
332 |     }
333 | 
334 |     // Interfaces
335 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
336 |     // to the interface instance.
337 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
338 |     FunctionType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
339 | 
340 |     if (getConstructor() != null && getConstructor().isInterface()) {
341 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
342 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
343 |           return true;
344 |         }
345 |       }
346 |     } else if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
347 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
348 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
349 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
350 |           return true;
351 |         }
352 |       }
353 |     }
354 | 
355 |     // other prototype based objects
356 |     if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
357 |       // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
358 |       // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
359 |       // to avoid guessing.
360 |       return true;
361 |     }
362 |     return thatObj != null && isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
363 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ComposeWarningsGuard.addGuard(WarningsGuard)

Original range: 86-101
Documentation:

Method code:
86 |   void addGuard(WarningsGuard guard) {
87 |     if (guard instanceof ComposeWarningsGuard) {
88 |       ComposeWarningsGuard composeGuard = (ComposeWarningsGuard) guard;
89 |       if (composeGuard.demoteErrors) {
90 |         this.demoteErrors = composeGuard.demoteErrors;
91 |       }
92 | 
93 |       // Reverse the guards, so that they have the same order in the result.
94 |       addGuards(Lists.newArrayList(composeGuard.guards.descendingSet()));
95 |     } else {
96 |       numberOfAdds++;
97 |       orderOfAddition.put(guard, numberOfAdds);
98 |       guards.remove(guard);
99 |       guards.add(guard);
100 |     }
101 |   }

/** Set the prototype without doing any sanity checks. */
Method code:
444 |   private boolean setPrototypeNoCheck(ObjectType prototype, Node propertyNode) {
445 |     ObjectType oldPrototype = prototypeSlot == null
446 |         ? null : (ObjectType) prototypeSlot.getType();
447 |     boolean replacedPrototype = oldPrototype != null;
448 | 
449 |     this.prototypeSlot = new Property("prototype", prototype, true,
450 |         propertyNode == null ? source : propertyNode);
451 |     prototype.setOwnerFunction(this);
452 | 
453 |     if (oldPrototype != null) {
454 |       // Disassociating the old prototype makes this easier to debug--
455 |       // we don't have to worry about two prototypes running around.
456 |       oldPrototype.setOwnerFunction(null);
457 |     }
458 | 
459 |     if (isConstructor() || isInterface()) {
460 |       FunctionType superClass = getSuperClassConstructor();
461 |       if (superClass != null) {
462 |         superClass.addSubType(this);
463 |       }
464 | 
465 |       if (isInterface()) {
466 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getExtendedInterfaces()) {
467 |           if (interfaceType.getConstructor() != null) {
468 |             interfaceType.getConstructor().addSubType(this);
469 |           }
470 |         }
471 |       }
472 |     }
473 | 
474 |     if (replacedPrototype) {
475 |       clearCachedValues();
476 |     }
477 | 
478 |     return true;
479 |   }

   * Detaches child from Node and replaces it with newChild.

Method code:
711 |   public void replaceChild(Node child, Node newChild) {
712 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(newChild.next == null,
713 |         "The new child node has siblings.");
714 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(newChild.parent == null,
715 |         "The new child node already has a parent.");
716 |

718 |     newChild.copyInformationFrom(child);
719 |

721 |     newChild.parent = this;
722 |     if (child == first) {
723 |       first = newChild;
724 |     } else {
725 |       Node prev = getChildBefore(child);
726 |       prev.next = newChild;
727 |     }
728 |     if (child == last) {
729 |       last = newChild;
730 |     }
731 |     child.next = null;
732 |     child.parent = null;

===== M0563 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1191-1199
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1191 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1192 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1193 |       return -1;
1194 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1195 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1196 |     } else {
1197 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1198 |     }
1199 |   }

===== M0570 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.parseInputs()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1295-1419
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the externs and main inputs.
   *
   * @return A synthetic root node whose two children are the externs root
   *     and the main root
   */
Method code:
1295 |   Node parseInputs() {
1296 |     boolean devMode = options.devMode != DevMode.OFF;
1297 | 
1298 |     // If old roots exist (we are parsing a second time), detach each of the
1299 |     // individual file parse trees.
1300 |     if (externsRoot != null) {
1301 |       externsRoot.detachChildren();
1302 |     }
1303 |     if (jsRoot != null) {
1304 |       jsRoot.detachChildren();
1305 |     }
1306 | 
1307 |     // Parse main JS sources.
1308 |     jsRoot = IR.block();
1309 |     jsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1310 | 
1311 |     externsRoot = IR.block();
1312 |     externsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1313 | 
1314 |     externAndJsRoot = IR.block(externsRoot, jsRoot);
1315 |     externAndJsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1316 | 
1317 |     if (options.tracer.isOn()) {
1318 |       tracker = new PerformanceTracker(jsRoot, options.tracer);
1319 |       addChangeHandler(tracker.getCodeChangeHandler());
1320 |     }
1321 | 
1322 |     Tracer tracer = newTracer(PARSING_PASS_NAME);
1323 | 
1324 |     try {
1325 |       // Parse externs sources.
1326 |       for (CompilerInput input : externs) {
1327 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1328 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1329 |           return null;
1330 |         }
1331 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1332 |       }
1333 | 
1334 |       // Modules inferred in ProcessCommonJS pass.
1335 |       if (options.transformAMDToCJSModules || options.processCommonJSModules) {
1336 |         processAMDAndCommonJSModules();
1337 |       }
1338 | 
1339 |       hoistExterns(externsRoot);
1340 | 
1341 |       // Check if the sources need to be re-ordered.
1342 |       boolean staleInputs = false;
1343 |       if (options.dependencyOptions.needsManagement()) {
1344 |         for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1345 |           // Forward-declare all the provided types, so that they
1346 |           // are not flagged even if they are dropped from the process.
1347 |           for (String provide : input.getProvides()) {
1348 |             getTypeRegistry().forwardDeclareType(provide);
1349 |           }
1350 |         }
1351 | 
1352 |         try {
1353 |           inputs =
1354 |               (moduleGraph == null ? new JSModuleGraph(modules) : moduleGraph)
1355 |               .manageDependencies(options.dependencyOptions, inputs);
1356 |           staleInputs = true;
1357 |         } catch (CircularDependencyException e) {
1358 |           report(JSError.make(
1359 |               JSModule.CIRCULAR_DEPENDENCY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1360 |         } catch (MissingProvideException e) {
1361 |           report(JSError.make(
1362 |               MISSING_ENTRY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1363 |         } catch (JSModuleGraph.MissingModuleException e) {
1364 |           report(JSError.make(
1365 |               MISSING_MODULE_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1366 |         }
1367 | 
1368 |         // If in IDE mode, we ignore the error and keep going.
1369 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1370 |           return null;
1371 |         }
1372 |       }
1373 | 
1374 |       hoistNoCompileFiles();
1375 | 
1376 |       if (staleInputs) {
1377 |         repartitionInputs();
1378 |       }
1379 | 
1380 |       // Build the AST.
1381 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1382 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1383 |         if (n == null) {
1384 |           continue;
1385 |         }
1386 | 
1387 |         if (devMode) {
1388 |           runSanityCheck();
1389 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1390 |             return null;
1391 |           }
1392 |         }
1393 | 
1394 |         // TODO(johnlenz): we shouldn't need to check both isExternExportsEnabled and
1395 |         // externExportsPath.
1396 |         if (options.sourceMapOutputPath != null ||
1397 |             options.nameReferenceReportPath != null ||
1398 |             options.isExternExportsEnabled() ||
1399 |             options.externExportsPath != null) {
1400 | 
1401 |           // Annotate the nodes in the tree with information from the
1402 |           // input file. This information is used to construct the SourceMap.
1403 |           SourceInformationAnnotator sia =
1404 |               new SourceInformationAnnotator(
1405 |                   input.getName(), options.devMode != DevMode.OFF);
1406 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(this, n, sia);
1407 |         }
1408 | 
1409 |         jsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1410 |       }
1411 | 
1412 |       if (hasErrors()) {
1413 |         return null;
1414 |       }
1415 |       return externAndJsRoot;
1416 |     } finally {
1417 |       stopTracer(tracer, PARSING_PASS_NAME);
1418 |     }
1419 |   }

===== M0573 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.add(Node,Context)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 103-757
Documentation:

Method code:
103 |   void add(Node n, Context context) {
104 |     if (!cc.continueProcessing()) {
105 |       return;
106 |     }
107 | 
108 |     int type = n.getType();
109 |     String opstr = NodeUtil.opToStr(type);
110 |     int childCount = n.getChildCount();
111 |     Node first = n.getFirstChild();
112 |     Node last = n.getLastChild();
113 | 
114 |     // Handle all binary operators
115 |     if (opstr != null && first != last) {
116 |       Preconditions.checkState(
117 |           childCount == 2,
118 |           "Bad binary operator \"%s\": expected 2 arguments but got %s",
119 |           opstr, childCount);
120 |       int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
121 | 
122 |       // For right-hand-side of operations, only pass context if it's
123 |       // the IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE one.
124 |       Context rhsContext = getContextForNoInOperator(context);
125 | 
126 |       // Handle associativity.
127 |       // e.g. if the parse tree is a * (b * c),
128 |       // we can simply generate a * b * c.
129 |       if (last.getType() == type &&
130 |           NodeUtil.isAssociative(type)) {
131 |         addExpr(first, p, context);
132 |         cc.addOp(opstr, true);
133 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
134 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(n) && NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(last)) {
135 |         // Assignments are the only right-associative binary operators
136 |         addExpr(first, p, context);
137 |         cc.addOp(opstr, true);
138 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
139 |       } else {
140 |         unrollBinaryOperator(n, type, opstr, context, rhsContext, p, p + 1);
141 |       }
142 |       return;
143 |     }
144 | 
145 |     cc.startSourceMapping(n);
146 | 
147 |     switch (type) {
148 |       case Token.TRY: {
149 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getNext().isBlock() &&
150 |                 !first.getNext().hasMoreThanOneChild());
151 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount >= 2 && childCount <= 3);
152 | 
153 |         add("try");
154 |         add(first, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
155 | 
156 |         // second child contains the catch block, or nothing if there
157 |         // isn't a catch block
158 |         Node catchblock = first.getNext().getFirstChild();
159 |         if (catchblock != null) {
160 |           add(catchblock);
161 |         }
162 | 
163 |         if (childCount == 3) {
164 |           add("finally");
165 |           add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
166 |         }
167 |         break;
168 |       }
169 | 
170 |       case Token.CATCH:
171 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
172 |         add("catch(");
173 |         add(first);
174 |         add(")");
175 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
176 |         break;
177 | 
178 |       case Token.THROW:
179 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
180 |         add("throw");
181 |         add(first);
182 | 
183 |         // Must have a ';' after a throw statement, otherwise safari can't
184 |         // parse this.
185 |         cc.endStatement(true);
186 |         break;
187 | 
188 |       case Token.RETURN:
189 |         add("return");
190 |         if (childCount == 1) {
191 |           add(first);
192 |         } else {
193 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
194 |         }
195 |         cc.endStatement();
196 |         break;
197 | 
198 |       case Token.VAR:
199 |         if (first != null) {
200 |           add("var ");
201 |           addList(first, false, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
202 |         }
203 |         break;
204 | 
205 |       case Token.LABEL_NAME:
206 |         Preconditions.checkState(!n.getString().isEmpty());
207 |         addIdentifier(n.getString());
208 |         break;
209 | 
210 |       case Token.NAME:
211 |         if (first == null || first.isEmpty()) {
212 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
213 |         } else {
214 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
215 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
216 |           cc.addOp("=", true);
217 |           if (first.isComma()) {
218 |             addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.ASSIGN), Context.OTHER);
219 |           } else {
220 |             // Add expression, consider nearby code at lowest level of
221 |             // precedence.
222 |             addExpr(first, 0, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
223 |           }
224 |         }
225 |         break;
226 | 
227 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
228 |         add("[");
229 |         addArrayList(first);
230 |         add("]");
231 |         break;
232 | 
233 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST:
234 |         add("(");
235 |         addList(first);
236 |         add(")");
237 |         break;
238 | 
239 |       case Token.COMMA:
240 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
241 |         unrollBinaryOperator(n, Token.COMMA, ",", context,
242 |             getContextForNoInOperator(context), 0, 0);
243 |         break;
244 | 
245 |       case Token.NUMBER:
246 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
247 |         cc.addNumber(n.getDouble());
248 |         break;
249 | 
250 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
251 |       case Token.VOID:
252 |       case Token.NOT:
253 |       case Token.BITNOT:
254 |       case Token.POS: {
255 |         // All of these unary operators are right-associative
256 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
257 |         cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
258 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), Context.OTHER);
259 |         break;
260 |       }
261 | 
262 |       case Token.NEG: {
263 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
264 | 
265 |         // It's important to our sanity checker that the code
266 |         // we print produces the same AST as the code we parse back.
267 |         // NEG is a weird case because Rhino parses "- -2" as "2".
268 |         if (n.getFirstChild().isNumber()) {
269 |           cc.addNumber(-n.getFirstChild().getDouble());
270 |         } else {
271 |           cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
272 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), Context.OTHER);
273 |         }
274 | 
275 |         break;
276 |       }
277 | 
278 |       case Token.HOOK: {
279 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
280 |         int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
281 |         addExpr(first, p + 1, context);
282 |         cc.addOp("?", true);
283 |         addExpr(first.getNext(), 1, Context.OTHER);
284 |         cc.addOp(":", true);
285 |         addExpr(last, 1, Context.OTHER);
286 |         break;
287 |       }
288 | 
289 |       case Token.REGEXP:
290 |         if (!first.isString() ||
291 |             !last.isString()) {
292 |           throw new Error("Expected children to be strings");
293 |         }
294 | 
295 |         String regexp = regexpEscape(first.getString(), outputCharsetEncoder);
296 | 
297 |         // I only use one .add because whitespace matters
298 |         if (childCount == 2) {
299 |           add(regexp + last.getString());
300 |         } else {
301 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
302 |           add(regexp);
303 |         }
304 |         break;
305 | 
306 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
307 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
308 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
309 |         }
310 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
311 |         boolean funcNeedsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
312 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
313 |           add("(");
314 |         }
315 | 
316 |         add("function");
317 |         add(first);
318 | 
319 |         add(first.getNext());
320 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
321 |         cc.endFunction(context == Context.STATEMENT);
322 | 
323 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
324 |           add(")");
325 |         }
326 |         break;
327 | 
328 |       case Token.GETTER_DEF:
329 |       case Token.SETTER_DEF:
330 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getParent().isObjectLit());
331 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
332 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.isFunction());
333 | 
334 |         // Get methods are unnamed
335 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getFirstChild().getString().isEmpty());
336 |         if (type == Token.GETTER_DEF) {
337 |           // Get methods have no parameters.
338 |           Preconditions.checkState(!first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasChildren());
339 |           add("get ");
340 |         } else {
341 |           // Set methods have one parameter.
342 |           Preconditions.checkState(first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasOneChild());
343 |           add("set ");
344 |         }
345 | 
346 |         // The name is on the GET or SET node.
347 |         String name = n.getString();
348 |         Node fn = first;
349 |         Node parameters = fn.getChildAtIndex(1);
350 |         Node body = fn.getLastChild();
351 | 
352 |         // Add the property name.
353 |         if (!n.isQuotedString() &&
354 |             TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(name) &&
355 |             // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that were
356 |             // Unicode escaped.
357 |             NodeUtil.isLatin(name)) {
358 |           add(name);
359 |         } else {
360 |           // Determine if the string is a simple number.
361 |           double d = getSimpleNumber(name);
362 |           if (!Double.isNaN(d)) {
363 |             cc.addNumber(d);
364 |           } else {
365 |             addJsString(n);
366 |           }
367 |         }
368 | 
369 |         add(parameters);
370 |         add(body, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
371 |         break;
372 | 
373 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
374 |       case Token.BLOCK: {
375 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
376 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
377 |         }
378 |         boolean preserveBlock = context == Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK;
379 |         if (preserveBlock) {
380 |           cc.beginBlock();
381 |         }
382 | 
383 |         boolean preferLineBreaks =
384 |             type == Token.SCRIPT ||
385 |             (type == Token.BLOCK &&
386 |                 !preserveBlock &&
387 |                 n.getParent() != null &&
388 |                 n.getParent().isScript());
389 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
390 |           add(c, Context.STATEMENT);
391 | 
392 |           // VAR doesn't include ';' since it gets used in expressions
393 |           if (c.isVar()) {
394 |             cc.endStatement();
395 |           }
396 | 
397 |           if (c.isFunction()) {
398 |             cc.maybeLineBreak();
399 |           }
400 | 
401 |           // Prefer to break lines in between top-level statements
402 |           // because top-level statements are more homogeneous.
403 |           if (preferLineBreaks) {
404 |             cc.notePreferredLineBreak();
405 |           }
406 |         }
407 |         if (preserveBlock) {
408 |           cc.endBlock(cc.breakAfterBlockFor(n, context == Context.STATEMENT));
409 |         }
410 |         break;
411 |       }
412 | 
413 |       case Token.FOR:
414 |         if (childCount == 4) {
415 |           add("for(");
416 |           if (first.isVar()) {
417 |             add(first, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
418 |           } else {
419 |             addExpr(first, 0, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
420 |           }
421 |           add(";");
422 |           add(first.getNext());
423 |           add(";");
424 |           add(first.getNext().getNext());
425 |           add(")");
426 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
427 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
428 |         } else {
429 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
430 |           add("for(");
431 |           add(first);
432 |           add("in");
433 |           add(first.getNext());
434 |           add(")");
435 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
436 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
437 |         }
438 |         break;
439 | 
440 |       case Token.DO:
441 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
442 |         add("do");
443 |         addNonEmptyStatement(first, Context.OTHER, false);
444 |         add("while(");
445 |         add(last);
446 |         add(")");
447 |         cc.endStatement();
448 |         break;
449 | 
450 |       case Token.WHILE:
451 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
452 |         add("while(");
453 |         add(first);
454 |         add(")");
455 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
456 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
457 |         break;
458 | 
459 |       case Token.EMPTY:
460 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
461 |         break;
462 | 
463 |       case Token.GETPROP: {
464 |         Preconditions.checkState(
465 |             childCount == 2,
466 |             "Bad GETPROP: expected 2 children, but got %s", childCount);
467 |         Preconditions.checkState(
468 |             last.isString(),
469 |             "Bad GETPROP: RHS should be STRING");
470 |         boolean needsParens = (first.isNumber());
471 |         if (needsParens) {
472 |           add("(");
473 |         }
474 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
475 |         if (needsParens) {
476 |           add(")");
477 |         }
478 |         add(".");
479 |         addIdentifier(last.getString());
480 |         break;
481 |       }
482 | 
483 |       case Token.GETELEM:
484 |         Preconditions.checkState(
485 |             childCount == 2,
486 |             "Bad GETELEM: expected 2 children but got %s", childCount);
487 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
488 |         add("[");
489 |         add(first.getNext());
490 |         add("]");
491 |         break;
492 | 
493 |       case Token.WITH:
494 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
495 |         add("with(");
496 |         add(first);
497 |         add(")");
498 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
499 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
500 |         break;
501 | 
502 |       case Token.INC:
503 |       case Token.DEC: {
504 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
505 |         String o = type == Token.INC ? "++" : "--";
506 |         int postProp = n.getIntProp(Node.INCRDECR_PROP);
507 |         // A non-zero post-prop value indicates a post inc/dec, default of zero
508 |         // is a pre-inc/dec.
509 |         if (postProp != 0) {
510 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
511 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
512 |         } else {
513 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
514 |           add(first);
515 |         }
516 |         break;
517 |       }
518 | 
519 |       case Token.CALL:
520 |         // We have two special cases here:
521 |         // 1) If the left hand side of the call is a direct reference to eval,
522 |         // then it must have a DIRECT_EVAL annotation. If it does not, then
523 |         // that means it was originally an indirect call to eval, and that
524 |         // indirectness must be preserved.
525 |         // 2) If the left hand side of the call is a property reference,
526 |         // then the call must not a FREE_CALL annotation. If it does, then
527 |         // that means it was originally an call without an explicit this and
528 |         // that must be preserved.
529 |         if (isIndirectEval(first)
530 |             || n.getBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL) && NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
531 |           add("(0,");
532 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.COMMA), Context.OTHER);
533 |           add(")");
534 |         } else {
535 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
536 |         }
537 |         add("(");
538 |         addList(first.getNext());
539 |         add(")");
540 |         break;
541 | 
542 |       case Token.IF:
543 |         boolean hasElse = childCount == 3;
544 |         boolean ambiguousElseClause =
545 |             context == Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE && !hasElse;
546 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
547 |           cc.beginBlock();
548 |         }
549 | 
550 |         add("if(");
551 |         add(first);
552 |         add(")");
553 | 
554 |         if (hasElse) {
555 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
556 |               first.getNext(), Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE, false);
557 |           add("else");
558 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
559 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
560 |         } else {
561 |           addNonEmptyStatement(first.getNext(), Context.OTHER, false);
562 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
563 |         }
564 | 
565 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
566 |           cc.endBlock();
567 |         }
568 |         break;
569 | 
570 |       case Token.NULL:
571 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
572 |         cc.addConstant("null");
573 |         break;
574 | 
575 |       case Token.THIS:
576 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
577 |         add("this");
578 |         break;
579 | 
580 |       case Token.FALSE:
581 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
582 |         cc.addConstant("false");
583 |         break;
584 | 
585 |       case Token.TRUE:
586 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
587 |         cc.addConstant("true");
588 |         break;
589 | 
590 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
591 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
592 |         add("continue");
593 |         if (childCount == 1) {
594 |           if (!first.isLabelName()) {
595 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
596 |           }
597 |           add(" ");
598 |           add(first);
599 |         }
600 |         cc.endStatement();
601 |         break;
602 | 
603 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
604 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
605 |         add("debugger");
606 |         cc.endStatement();
607 |         break;
608 | 
609 |       case Token.BREAK:
610 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
611 |         add("break");
612 |         if (childCount == 1) {
613 |           if (!first.isLabelName()) {
614 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
615 |           }
616 |           add(" ");
617 |           add(first);
618 |         }
619 |         cc.endStatement();
620 |         break;
621 | 
622 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
623 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
624 |         add(first, Context.START_OF_EXPR);
625 |         cc.endStatement();
626 |         break;
627 | 
628 |       case Token.NEW:
629 |         add("new ");
630 |         int precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
631 | 
632 |         // If the first child contains a CALL, then claim higher precedence
633 |         // to force parentheses. Otherwise, when parsed, NEW will bind to the
634 |         // first viable parentheses (don't traverse into functions).
635 |         if (NodeUtil.containsType(
636 |             first, Token.CALL, NodeUtil.MATCH_NOT_FUNCTION)) {
637 |           precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(first.getType()) + 1;
638 |         }
639 |         addExpr(first, precedence, Context.OTHER);
640 | 
641 |         // '()' is optional when no arguments are present
642 |         Node next = first.getNext();
643 |         if (next != null) {
644 |           add("(");
645 |           addList(next);
646 |           add(")");
647 |         }
648 |         break;
649 | 
650 |       case Token.STRING_KEY:
651 |         Preconditions.checkState(
652 |             childCount == 1, "Object lit key must have 1 child");
653 |         addJsString(n);
654 |         break;
655 | 
656 |       case Token.STRING:
657 |         Preconditions.checkState(
658 |             childCount == 0, "A string may not have children");
659 |         addJsString(n);
660 |         break;
661 | 
662 |       case Token.DELPROP:
663 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
664 |         add("delete ");
665 |         add(first);
666 |         break;
667 | 
668 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT: {
669 |         boolean needsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
670 |         if (needsParens) {
671 |           add("(");
672 |         }
673 |         add("{");
674 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
675 |           if (c != first) {
676 |             cc.listSeparator();
677 |           }
678 | 
679 |           if (c.isGetterDef() || c.isSetterDef()) {
680 |             add(c);
681 |           } else {
682 |             Preconditions.checkState(c.isStringKey());
683 |             String key = c.getString();
684 |             // Object literal property names don't have to be quoted if they
685 |             // are not JavaScript keywords
686 |             if (!c.isQuotedString() &&
687 |                 !TokenStream.isKeyword(key) &&
688 |                 TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(key) &&
689 |                 // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that
690 |                 // were Unicode escaped.
691 |                 NodeUtil.isLatin(key)) {
692 |               add(key);
693 |             } else {
694 |               // Determine if the string is a simple number.
695 |               double d = getSimpleNumber(key);
696 |               if (!Double.isNaN(d)) {
697 |                 cc.addNumber(d);
698 |               } else {
699 |                 addExpr(c, 1, Context.OTHER);
700 |               }
701 |             }
702 |             add(":");
703 |             addExpr(c.getFirstChild(), 1, Context.OTHER);
704 |           }
705 |         }
706 |         add("}");
707 |         if (needsParens) {
708 |           add(")");
709 |         }
710 |         break;
711 |       }
712 | 
713 |       case Token.SWITCH:
714 |         add("switch(");
715 |         add(first);
716 |         add(")");
717 |         cc.beginBlock();
718 |         addAllSiblings(first.getNext());
719 |         cc.endBlock(context == Context.STATEMENT);
720 |         break;
721 | 
722 |       case Token.CASE:
723 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
724 |         add("case ");
725 |         add(first);
726 |         addCaseBody(last);
727 |         break;
728 | 
729 |       case Token.DEFAULT_CASE:
730 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
731 |         add("default");
732 |         addCaseBody(first);
733 |         break;
734 | 
735 |       case Token.LABEL:
736 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
737 |         if (!first.isLabelName()) {
738 |           throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
739 |         }
740 |         add(first);
741 |         add(":");
742 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
743 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), true);
744 |         break;
745 | 
746 |       case Token.CAST:
747 |         add("(");
748 |         add(first);
749 |         add(")");
750 |         break;
751 | 
752 |       default:
753 |         throw new Error("Unknown type " + type + "\n" + n.toStringTree());
754 |     }
755 | 
756 |     cc.endSourceMapping(n);
757 |   }

Documentation:
/**
   * For each message such as "Not a good use of {0}", replace the place
   * holder {0} with a wild card that matches all possible strings.
   * Also put the any non-place-holder in quotes for regex matching later.
   */
Method code:
73 |   private Pattern replacePlaceHolders(String s) {
74 |     s = Pattern.quote(s);
75 |     return Pattern.compile(s.replaceAll("\\{\\d+\\}", "\\\\E.*\\\\Q"));
76 |   }

===== M0585 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 672-684
Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
672 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, JSType type) {
673 |     UnionTypeBuilder typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
674 |     if (typeSet == null) {
675 |       typeSet = new UnionTypeBuilder(this, PROPERTY_CHECKING_UNION_SIZE);
676 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
677 |     }
678 | 
679 |     typeSet.addAlternate(type);
680 |     addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, type);
681 | 
682 |     // Clear cached values that depend on typesIndexedByProperty.
683 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
684 |   }

/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
112 |   public static ParseResult parse(StaticSourceFile sourceFile,
113 |                                   String sourceString,
114 |                                   Config config,
115 |                                   ErrorReporter errorReporter,
116 |                                   Logger logger) throws IOException {
117 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
118 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
119 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
120 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
121 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
122 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
123 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
124 | 
125 |     // ES5 specifically allows trailing commas
126 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(
127 |         config.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3);
128 | 
129 |     // Do our own identifier check for ECMASCRIPT 5
130 |     boolean acceptEs5 =
131 |         config.isIdeMode || config.languageMode != LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3;
132 |     compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(acceptEs5);
133 | 
134 |     compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(false);
135 |     compilerEnv.setIdeMode(config.isIdeMode);
136 |     compilerEnv.setRecoverFromErrors(config.isIdeMode);
137 | 
138 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
139 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
140 |     try {
141 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceFile.getName(), 1);
142 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
143 |       logger.info(
144 |           "Error parsing " + sourceFile.getName() + ": " + e.getMessage());
145 |     } finally {
146 |       Context.exit();
147 |     }
148 |     Node root = null;
149 |     if (astRoot != null) {
150 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
151 |           astRoot, sourceFile, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
152 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
153 |     }
154 |     return new ParseResult(root, astRoot);
155 |   }
